# Sandbox manifest: session_01VM97q6A98GgtoPKCamoiT6

Every file this builder session produced in its container outside the repo checkouts, as of 2026-10-04 about 19:30 Melbourne time. "committed" files are in this folder under the same relative path: `scratchpad/` is the session scratchpad, and `tmp/` is `/tmp`.

## Patches (local claude/switch-1, never merged)

- `patches/0001-SWITCH-1-e2e-the-worker-unit-must-carry-OOMScoreAdju.patch` is 217e422, which made the e2e OOM process check conditional. The supervisor ruled the strict check stays, so it is not to be merged.
- 84a403a is only a merge of base 9f7ad71 into 217e422 with no content of its own, so it has no patch. To regenerate it, run `git merge 9f7ad71` on top of 217e422.

## Excluded without listing each file

- `/tmp/chain-volume-*`, `/tmp/bt-*` and other test temp directories are created by vitest runs (`pnpm test`). Regenerate them by running the tests.
- The worktrees `/tmp/wt-api1`, `/tmp/wt-drill`, `/tmp/wt-before` and `/tmp/wt-hand` are checkouts of pushed branches (claude/api-1 62242b3, claude/e2e-drill 2575ee1, detached 669de71, claude/handover-notes), plus node_modules.
- `/tmp/wt-before` also carries a local, uncommitted edit to ops/test/e2e.sh that skips the /proc OOM line, used only for the local evidence run.
- Docker images and containers (`zeroed-e2e`) are built by ops/test/e2e.sh.

## Results kept here

- `tmp/e2eb/logs/summary.txt` is base 669de71 on the push path: FAIL "the drill endpoint is not on".
- `tmp/e2e2/logs/summary.txt` is the fix d4b98b1: all checks pass.
- `scratchpad/check*.log` and `*-check.out` are pnpm check outputs from the session.
